## 1. 文案

# 文案

* [ ] 增长/产品同事先优化黄色部分（Title, description, H1, H2）

* [ ] 其次优化紫色部分（H3）及正文的词频

* [ ] 首页Copy优化注意事项辛苦查漏补缺@kostja

* [x] Q: 静态页面多久修改一次不会影响SEO？
A：都不会英系那个

* [x] Q: 静态页面修改后，60分改到了80分还是30分，多久会看到效果？ 
A: 看页面曝光数量，网站有流量的话一两天就有变化，新网站一两周也ok

@kostja建议：

1. 先有一个60分的（符合SEO结构）之后再修改到80分

2. 提问@kostja具体从60分提升到90分的首页内容，有哪些todo？

首页Copy优化注意事项：

1. H2, H3标题的开头需要尽可能多出现我们要打的【核心关键词】（大词、流量大的词），可以用semantic keywords（同义词、替代词）

2. 关于业务的描述要我们用更准确的业务语言描述、包括考虑英语市场的常用词习惯。（例如gen-ai，genai，gen ai在北美市场很常说，其他市场统称ai）

3. 检查全页面，核心的1-5个关键词的出现率最好≥1%

4. 所有的中文’要改成英文' （AI生成的水印之一）

5. @kostja检查一下是否有其他具体的【优化要求】；上面任务不重要的地方也可以删改

6. Ad和variation词频率3%左右

## 广告素材生成页

Meta Title：AI Ad Generator: Automated Ads for Meta, Google & TikTok | Soku

Meta Description：Automate ad creative generation with Soku's AI Ad Generator. Produce text, images, short and long-form videos tailored to your audience for maximum ROI. Start free trial today.

> 记得所有link全用小写permalink: https://soku.ai/ai-ad-generator
> （关键词具体内容可以根据搜索量和匹配度替换）
> 
> 



**H1：Soku AI Ad Generator: Revolutionize Your Ad Creative Workflow **

**H2：What Is the AI Ad Generator? **

Soku's AI Ad Generator is your all-in-one solution for creating high-performing advertising materials across platforms like Meta, Google, and TikTok. With just a few inputs, the AI produces ready-to-launch creatives, including:

- **Text Ads**: Compelling headlines and descriptions optimized for clicks.  

- **Image Ads**: Professionally designed visuals aligned with your brand identity.  

- **Short Videos**: 15-second engaging clips for stories and reels.  

- **Long-Form Videos**: Demo or explainer videos tailored to your target audience.

**H2: Why Automate Ad Creative Production?**

**H3: The Challenges of Manual Ad Creation**

- **Time-Consuming**: Traditional workflows take weeks to produce a single campaign.  

- **Costly**: Hiring designers, copywriters, and video editors adds up quickly.  

- **Inconsistent Results**: Human errors lead to misaligned branding and underperforming creatives.

**H3: How Soku Solves These Pain Points**

- **Speed**: Generate multiple ad variations in under 3 minutes.  

- **Cost Efficiency**: Replace costly creative teams with AI-powered automation.  

- **Performance**: AI optimizes creatives based on real-time data for better engagement.



**H2: Key Features of Soku’s AI Ad Generator**

**H3: 🖌️ Intelligent Creative Generation**

- **Automated Design**: AI crafts visuals that highlight your product features and match your brand identity.  

- **Dynamic Copywriting**: Generates text tailored to your audience's preferences and platform requirements.  

- **Multi-Variant Testing**: Creates multiple versions of ads for A/B testing to identify top performers.  

**H3: 🎥 Video Production Made Simple**

- **Short-Form Videos**: Perfect for TikTok and Instagram stories, optimized for engagement.  

- **Long-Form Content**: Explainer videos designed to convert leads into customers.  

- **AI Storyboarding**: Automatically selects the best scenes and sequences for your video content.  

**H3: 📊 Performance Optimization**

- **Predictive Analytics**: AI evaluates potential performance before launch.  

- **Real-Time Adjustments**: Automatically retires underperforming creatives and scales winning on
